{"forms":[{"form":"spectra","tags":["plural"]},{"form":"spectrums","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A range; a continuous, infinite, one-dimensional set, possibly bounded by extremes."],"synonyms":[{"word":"sliding scale"},{"word":"continuum"}]},{"glosses":["Specifically, a range of colours representing light (electromagnetic radiation) of contiguous frequencies; hence electromagnetic spectrum, visible spectrum, ultraviolet spectrum, etc."]},{"glosses":["The autism spectrum."],"raw_tags":["with the"],"tags":["usually"],"topics":["education","human-sciences","psychology","sciences"]},{"glosses":["A set of frequencies that are used for radio-frequency communication; not necessarily a continuous range."],"topics":["communications","electrical-engineering","engineering","natural-sciences","physical-sciences","telecommunications"]},{"glosses":["The pattern of absorption or emission of radiation produced by a substance when subjected to energy (radiation, heat, electricity, etc.)."],"topics":["chemistry","natural-sciences","physical-sciences"]},{"glosses":["The set of eigenvalues of a matrix."],"synonyms":[{"word":"eigenspectrum"}],"topics":["linear-algebra","mathematics","sciences"]},{"glosses":["Of a bounded linear operator A, the set of scalar values λ such that the operator A—λI, where I denotes the identity operator, does not have a bounded inverse; intended as a generalisation of the linear algebra sense."],"qualifier":"functional analysis","topics":["mathematics","sciences"]},{"glosses":["An abstract object in mathematics created from a commutative ring R and denoted operatorname Spec(R) or operatorname SpecR and said to be the spectrum of R; useful in the study of such rings for providing a geometric object which encodes many of the properties R, and in modern geometry for generalizing the notion of an algebraic variety to that of an affine scheme. Formally, the set of all prime ideals R equipped with the Zariski topology and augmented with a sheaf of rings called the structure sheaf, generated by the B-sheaf on the distinguished open sets D_f which assigns the localization of R at f to each set D_f, regarded as a ring of functions on D_f. See Spectrum of a ring on Wikipedia.Wikipedia"],"qualifier":"commutative algebra","topics":["algebraic-geometry","geometry","mathematics","sciences"]},{"glosses":["Specter, apparition."],"tags":["obsolete"]},{"glosses":["The image of something seen that persists after the eyes are closed."]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈspɛktɹəm/"},{"ipa":"[ˈspɛktɹ̝̊əm] ~ [ˈspɛktɹ̝̊m̩]"},{"ipa":"[ˈspɛkt̠ɹ̠̊˔əm] ~ [ˈspɛkt̠ɹ̠̊˔m̩]"}],"upstream_line":147003,"upstream_offset":658694234,"upstream_record_sha256":"9d4123517627214f6c6721e1338f7be8b7dcc150b93f6191123c5e0daba668a4","word":"spectrum"}

{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"taxes","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"antonyms":[{"word":"subsidy"}],"glosses":["Money or goods collected by a government (or an entity to whom the government has delegated this power, e.g. in tax farming) to fund itself and its services, for example by levying a charge on income, purchases (sales), property or harvest, other than that money which is collected by the government in exchange for specific goods (e.g. the purchase of surplus vehicles)."],"synonyms":[{"word":"impost"},{"word":"tribute"},{"word":"contribution"},{"word":"duty"},{"word":"toll"},{"word":"rate"},{"word":"assessment"},{"word":"exaction"},{"word":"custom"},{"word":"demand"},{"word":"levy"}],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["A charge (of money, food, labor, etc) collected by a person, organization, etc; something required (exacted) from someone who is (really or notionally) under the control of the taxer, such as a contribution or service."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"a heavy tax on time or health","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A burdensome demand"],"tags":["figuratively","uncountable"]}],"sounds":[{"enpr":"tăks"},{"ipa":"/tæks/"}],"upstream_line":7472,"upstream_offset":47493177,"upstream_record_sha256":"049d97d4af9cafa4e7340147bd80c390e47b081cf63b3c106afa6edb2615d65d","word":"tax"}
